- **rebase**: Drive an intent-aware rebase, resolving conflicts by reading both sides' history. Use when the user invokes /rebase or $rebase in Grok Build with a rebase target, or asks to continue or abort a rebase in progress. ([telegramdesktop/tdesktop](https://github.com/telegramdesktop/tdesktop/blob/HEAD/.grok/commands/rebase.md) · ★34k · 24 copies)
- **rebase**: Drive an intent-aware rebase, resolving conflicts by reading both sides' history ([telegramdesktop/tdesktop](https://github.com/telegramdesktop/tdesktop/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/rebase.md) · ★34k · 25 copies)
- **rebase**: Rebase a worktree branch onto its base branch ([raine/workmux](https://github.com/raine/workmux/blob/HEAD/docs/src/content/docs/reference/commands/rebase.md) · ★3.1k · 2 copies)
- **rebase**: Rebase the current feature branch onto the latest upstream branch and resolve conflicts. ([rohitg00/awesome-claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/rohitg00/awesome-claude-code-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/update-branch/commands/rebase.md) · ★2.8k · 12 copies)
- **rebase**: Data-preserving git rebase and conflict resolution onto v2.1 (or any target branch) ([NVIDIA-AI-Blueprints/video-search-and-summarization](https://github.com/NVIDIA-AI-Blueprints/video-search-and-summarization/blob/HEAD/services/vios/.claude/commands/rebase.md) · ★2k · 2 copies)
- **rebase**: `grit rebase` replays commits reachable from `HEAD` but not from `&lt;upstream&gt;` onto `&lt;upstream&gt;` (or onto `--onto` when set). It uses the same history engine as [`grit history`](history.md). When every commit replays cleanly, grit writes new commit objects and updates refs in one batch. ([gitbutlerapp/grit](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/grit/blob/HEAD/content/docs/commands/rebase.md) · ★598)
- **rebase**: /ai-sdlc rebase &lt;pr-number&gt; — auto-rebase a PR onto latest main, resolve mechanical conflicts, force-push with --force-with-lease. ([ai-sdlc-framework/ai-sdlc](https://github.com/ai-sdlc-framework/ai-sdlc/blob/HEAD/ai-sdlc-plugin/commands/rebase.md) · ★447)
- **rebase**: Rebase OpenShift fork of an upstream repository to a new upstream release. ([openshift-eng/ai-helpers](https://github.com/openshift-eng/ai-helpers/blob/HEAD/plugins/openshift/commands/rebase.md) · ★123)
- **rebase**: Rebase current branch onto a target, resolving conflicts correctly ([contember/okena](https://github.com/contember/okena/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/rebase.md) · ★104)
- **rebase**: Reapply commits on top of another base tip. ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/rebase.md) · ★101)
- **rebase**: Each replayed revision is recorded through the ChangeRevisionBuilder. Rebase edges use the typed `rebase` relation and preserve the logical Change ID across rewrites. Multi-parent `squash` and multi-result `split` edges are represented as ordered sidecar predecessor rows; no Change ID is inserted into a Git header. AI links use redacted stable operation/Change IDs, never replayed commit OIDs. ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/rebase.md) · ★101)
- **rebase**: Rebase current branch onto trunk (origin/master or origin/main), predict and resolve conflicts ([tony/claude-code-riper-5](https://github.com/tony/claude-code-riper-5/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/rebase.md) · ★96 · 4 copies)
- **rebase**: Rebase current branch onto main and fix merge conflicts ([UT-ADL/autoware_mini](https://github.com/UT-ADL/autoware_mini/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/rebase.md) · ★93)
- **rebase**: Rebase the current branch onto origin/main. ([malbeclabs/doublezero](https://github.com/malbeclabs/doublezero/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/rebase.md) · ★51)
- **rebase**: /heavy I want to Git rebase the current working tree onto the $ARGUMENTS branch. Use haskell-pro to analyze and resolve these conflicts in a way that preserves the semantics of the incoming changes, while maintaining the intent of the current working tree's work. Continue until the branch is fully rebased. ([HIDORAKAI002/ai-workspace-archive](https://github.com/HIDORAKAI002/ai-workspace-archive/blob/HEAD/ai_skills/sources/promptdeploy/commands/rebase.md) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **rebase**: Rebase current branch onto a feature branch ([ourines/worktree-task-plugin](https://github.com/ourines/worktree-task-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/rebase.md) · ★39)
- **rebase**: Rebase the current stack onto an updated branch. ([mrmans0n/git-gud](https://github.com/mrmans0n/git-gud/blob/HEAD/docs/src/commands/rebase.md) · ★37)
